- **rocket-build**: You are the BUILDER. Your ONLY job is to build the feature, test it, and commit the code. You do NOT review, you do NOT ship, you do NOT touch the feature queue, the spec, the learnings file, or `SHIPPED.md`. The outer `rocket.sh` runs the reviewers, gates on PASS/FAIL, and performs the ship itself (in bash, deterministically). Stay in your lane. ([nealiyer88/nexus-finance](https://github.com/nealiyer88/nexus-finance/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rocket-build.md) · ★1)
- **rocket-deploy**: Publish a Rocket.net staging site to production with a scope review. ([juliandickie/rocket-net-plugin](https://github.com/juliandickie/rocket-net-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/rocket-deploy.md))
- **rocket-fix**: You are the FIXER. The reviewers FAILED the build. Your ONLY job is to fix the BLOCKING issues in the verdict, keep tests green, and commit. You do NOT re-review and you do NOT ship — `rocket.sh` re-runs the reviewers after you and decides. Stay in your lane. ([nealiyer88/nexus-finance](https://github.com/nealiyer88/nexus-finance/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/rocket-fix.md) · ★1)

- **run**: Run the design-review vitality pipeline on a target (file, route, component, story or email). ([davidgarciagordo/design-review](https://github.com/davidgarciagordo/design-review/blob/HEAD/commands/run.md))
- **run**: `peekaboo run` loads a `.peekaboo.json` (PeekabooScript) file, executes every step via `ProcessService`, and reports the aggregated result. It’s the same engine the agent runtime uses for scripted flows, which makes it ideal for regression suites or reproducing agent traces. ([dawsonblock/OracleOS](https://github.com/dawsonblock/OracleOS/blob/HEAD/Interface/Web/page-agent-main/Peekaboo-main/docs/commands/run.md))

- **run**: 开始一个 coding session。从功能清单中选最高优先级的未完成功能，实现、测试，提交并更新进度日志。每次调用推进一个功能。 ([death2233/long-running-agent](https://github.com/death2233/long-running-agent/blob/HEAD/commands/run.md))
